- [x] AG.4 The physics seam (design decision 3), on nothing in flight: avian3d 0.8 from crates.io in `guacamole`, or Avian's `main` at `1070759b2145` pinned by rev if 0.8 hasn't published when this box starts (the reason written here), never `34c9b008d2f4`; the new pure crate `crates/guac-motion` (no Bevy) holding the seam (a shape cast returning the time of impact and the contact planes there, contact manifolds within a distance at a pose, a ray cast returning the triangle it hit, overlaps) and its parry3d implementation over static trimeshes with internal-edge fixing and the four convex shapes; `guacamole`'s Avian implementation over `SpatialQuery`, with static trimesh colliders built with `FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES`, kinematic and sensor colliders and no dynamic body. Unit tests in this box, every case through BOTH implementations, Avian's in a headless app (`MinimalPlugins` and Avian's plugins, no renderer): a sphere cast onto a plane stops at the expected fraction; a corner yields two planes; a ray reports its triangle's index; a sphere sliding across the seam between two coplanar triangles never snags. Verify: record `.githooks/pre-push` cold and warm on the Mac beside AA.10's numbers, the load average beside them (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac: the seam landed, both sides answer the table alike to the bit. Pin: `cargo search avian3d` on 2026-10-19 still tops out at 0.7.0 (Bevy 0.19), so `guacamole` takes Avian's `main` at `1070759b2145dc078abedace91dc7fe78013a45c` ("Update to Bevy 0.20 (#1084)", avian3d 0.8.0-dev on parry3d 0.31.1 and `bevy_transform_interpolation` 0.6) by rev, features `3d` and `parry-f32` only, until 0.8 publishes. `crates/guac-motion` (pure, `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]`, parry3d 0.31 and glam 0.33, no Bevy): `seam` holds `PhysicsSeam`: `cast` (a convex shape swept along a motion, unturned: the fraction and meters to the first solid it moves into, and the contact planes within the distance asked at that pose, never less than `CAST_SLOP` 1e-3 m so the hit is among them), `contacts` (one plane per body and triangle within a distance, its deepest point: body, triangle, material, normal out of the body, point, signed gap), `ray` (body, triangle, material, distance, point, normal; both sides of a triangle; out of a convex it starts inside) and `overlaps` (sensors included), each with an exclude list; bodies static, kinematic or sensor (overlaps only); the four `CollisionInfo` shapes, box and cylinder rounded by a margin (Havok's convex radius); a `Mesh` with a material per triangle, refused by name when empty, its materials miscounted, an index out of range or a vertex not finite. `parry` holds `ParryWorld`, shapes built as Avian builds them (its `FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES` is parry's plus `ORIENTED`, its capsule +Y to −Y) and every query the parry call `SpatialQuery` makes with Avian's cast options (unit direction, origin penetration ignored), a per-body AABB cull; `set_pose` moves a kinematic body or sensor and refuses a static one. `guacamole`'s [`physics.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/physics.rs): `SeamPlugin` adds `PhysicsSchedulePlugin`, `ColliderHierarchyPlugin`, `ColliderBackendPlugin`, `ColliderTreePlugin` and `BroadPhaseCorePlugin` (its system sets only) and puts `SolverSystems::Finalize` in `PhysicsStepSystems::Solver`, no solver, narrow phase, pairs, joints, mass, interpolation or transform sync; the first try without the last two didn't build (Avian builds `PhysicsSchedule` with ambiguity detection as an error: 5 ambiguous pairs), and without the hierarchy plugin a body's collider never joined a tree (`update_moved_collider_aabbs` panicked on its placeholder index). `spawn_body`: the mesh `RigidBody::Static` on `Collider::trimesh_with_config(.., FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES)`, a placement `Kinematic`, a sensor `Kinematic` with `Sensor`, none dynamic, each posed by `Position` and `Rotation`; `AvianSeam`, a `SystemParam`: casts through `cast_shape_predicate`, rays through `cast_ray_predicate` with the triangle from the same ray on the hit collider's parry shape (`RayHitData` has no feature), contacts from `aabb_intersections_with_aabb` and parry's manifolds on `Collider::shape_scaled` (Avian's `contact_manifolds` drops the sub-shape, the triangle), overlaps through `shape_intersections`; a moved body shows once the physics schedule has run. Registered in the TV app (`mod physics`, `physics::SeamPlugin`; `dead_code` expected outside tests until AG.16 and AG.21 call it). What it adds to startup, a headless `MinimalPlugins` app with and without it: systems 11 → 25, observers 0 → 20, resources 13 → 30, schedules 8 → 10, the physics schedule run once a fixed step; building the app 4-21 ms and an empty update 0.34-0.83 ms either way in a debug build at a 1-minute load of ~40, noise over any difference; no other entity gets a physics component. Tolerances declared in `conformance.rs` before the first run: 1e-4 against a closed form (m, and per normal component), 1e-5 between the two sides, exact on bodies, triangles, materials, plane counts and overlap sets. The first run held 1e-4 on contacts and rays but not on casts: a sphere onto the rounded door stopped 1.57e-4 m past it (1.700157 for 1.7), so cast distances check to 1e-3 (`TOL_CAST`); a grazing cast across the seam from 5 mm inside stopped at 0.8890 m against the closed form's 0.9003 (1.2 mm of gap at an 8° graze), checked as a bound; and exactly along a face it started 0.1 m inside, a cast stopped at 0, float noise in the contact normal deciding, so that query became slightly-away and `cast`'s docs say it. Otherwise the casts sat within 2.0e-5 (sphere onto plane) and 1.3e-5 (the shapes) of closed form. Avian against parry: all 107 answers bit-identical, 87 planes among them, so none of the declared 1e-5 is used. The table, 16 cases, 107 queries, each case through both (`guac-motion`'s `the_table`, `guacamole`'s `the_table_through_avian_agrees_with_parry` in a headless `MinimalPlugins` app, no renderer, one 1/64 s fixed step an update): `sphere_cast_onto_plane` (fraction 0.5, the floor's triangle 1 and material 7 at the stop with no contact distance asked); `convex_shapes_onto_plane` (box with and without margin, turned 0.7 rad, capsule upright and on its side, cylinder with and without margin, each stopping on its lowest point); `corner_gives_two_planes` (a floor and wall in one mesh: a sphere in the corner gets two planes, +Y and −X, and a diagonal cast stops at 0.5 touching both); `ray_reports_its_triangle` (a 32-triangle soup at level 213's body translation, each triangle its own vertices so merging rewrites the index buffer: triangles 0, 5, 17 and 31 with their materials); `seam_never_snags` (63 poses across the seam of two coplanar triangles at the original's 0.01 m snap height, touching and 5 mm inside: every plane within 0.05 m is the faces' +Y; casts across the seam and along it at the snap height go all the way); `cast_inside_doesnt_weld` (the limit above, pinned: from inside, the cast stops on the far triangle's edge while the planes there are faces'); `kinematic_blocks` and `kinematic_moved` (a door turned 90° and moved 2 m); `sensors_only_overlap` (overlaps see a sensor, casts, rays and contacts pass it); `exclude_skips_bodies`; `start_inside` (into a wall: fraction 0 at −0.1; out, or along and slightly out: no stop); `contact_reach` (a 0.03 m gap in 0.05, not in 0.02); `deepest_point` (a tipped box: the plane at −0.01, not the high edge's +0.04); `mesh_back` (contacts one-sided: a sphere under the floor gets no plane; a cast up into it stops at 1.5 m with no plane at all, written into `Cast`'s and `Mesh`'s docs for AG.15's winding and AG.16); `ray_sides`; `zero_motion`. Beside it: `guac-motion` 5 tests (the table; a world built twice answers bit for bit; the floor without internal-edge fixing gives a sphere 5 mm inside it, just short of the seam, a plane tilted against its motion (normal x under −0.05; the seam case's pose 5 read −0.23 that way), fixed under 1e-6; `ParryWorld`'s and `Mesh`'s refusals); `guacamole` 6 (the table and agreement; a move shows after the physics step and not before; Avian's markers (static mesh with `FIX_INTERNAL_EDGES` and `ORIENTED`, kinematic, kinematic `Sensor`); `set_pose` refusing a static body; another entity's `Transform` untouched with no `Position`, `Rotation` or `ColliderAabb`; Avian's constructors and the parry shapes giving the same AABB for all six convex shapes). Each check deleted or bent in turn: on the Avian side 10, 9 failing the table and `Sensor` left off surviving it (the seam filters sensors by its own kind), so the markers test pins it; on the parry side 10, all failing once `deepest_point` caught the one that kept the shallowest point. Spec and design corrections below. **The rule** ([`guac_formats::level::at_start`](../../../crates/guac-formats/src/level.rs), `ActivityState`, `AtStart`): `_activityState` takes the EBOOT's `PLACEMENT_*` order, each value seen where its name says: the hero's own placement reads 0 (PLAYER); a level file holds only 4 (UNMANAGED) and 5 (TEMPLATE); at run time a 4 becomes 1 (ACTIVE: it has a corpus, the scene-graph node its model hangs under and the holder of its phantom), 2 (INACTIVE: no corpus, so no solid, and by AF.9's layout nothing drawn) or 3 (REMOVED). A template never runs itself; an UNMANAGED placement with an activation range is active exactly while the hero stands within that range of its `_initMatrix` position, measured in 3D, the range in feet; one without a range is its scripts' to wake, take off or leave. A layout-3 extract is rebuilt and a whole first run with the Mac's own ffmpeg takes 89.2 s: the roster ready at 2.58 s, the models 12.28 s, the level from 14.87 s for 10.79 s, ffmpeg looked for at 25.77 s, the intros 63.5 s, load average 12.4 at the end (the transcode); so the level stage puts 10.8-11.1 s ahead of a first run's intros (design decision 8's delay), and the next start finds it current: re-extracted once. AG.3's `giants_level` (3 tests, 37.7 s), AE.6's `giants_materials` (10.2 s), `giants_model_stage` (36.2 s; 211,315,108 bytes, 6,636 more than AG.14's for every material's empty `tracks`) and `giants_heroes` (7.64 s) rerun green. fmt, clippy -D warnings, `cargo test --workspace` (1,316 passed, 0 failed, 112 ignored; one earlier full run lost guacamole's `a_placement_with_no_profile_unlocked_has_no_owner` to a timeout at a load of 9.8, which passed alone and in the rerun) and `openspec validate --all --strict` green; e2e: phone built with `BUILD_TOKEN=e2e-test`, `E2E_APP=guacamole` 57 passed, 0 failed, 370.6 s at a 1-minute load of 7.2-16.6. Notes for later boxes: AG.16's welding type is in each collision mesh (4 on all 68); AG.19 draws tracks, multiply and the three other effects from these records and loads a 177 MB RON scene in dev (31 MB of postcard for the whole level); AG.25's rule decides whether a refused prop matters (the fairy gem's are templates); AG.33 carries IR v3, the level stage's parts and its cost into the design doc and CLAUDE.md. Review (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac): `giants_level_stage` run twice, 2:23 at a 1-minute load of 3.4-14.3 and 2:45 at 13.2-16.6 (other lanes beside it): 5 of 5 parts, 0 failed, the fairy gem the one prop refused; the stage 10.37 s and 11.07 s, 622 files written and 21 kept, 299,887,986 bytes, 638 textures of which 21 already in the store (`textures/` 890); every count in the tick the same; a resume 0.171-0.177 s; postcard 31,325,321 bytes; the layout-3 rebuild current after one run (the tick's "ffmpeg looked for at" is the index event, just after ffmpeg's locate and checks). Its collision check compared floats with `==`, which takes -0.0 for 0.0: now `f32::to_bits`, vertex for vertex and the radius, equal; and RON and postcard were compared on the scene and the collision only: now each of the five parts' RON, decoded and put in postcard, is its postcard file byte for byte. No ffmpeg, on the real dump: `guac-extract extract` under `env -i` (no PATH, no `GUAC_FFMPEG`) in a `sandbox-exec` profile denying reads of Homebrew's ffmpeg (the last folder it tries), 36.0 s at 6.9: the roster, 67 models and 67 heroes, then the level whole (5 parts, 17.72 s), then `GUAC-E-NO-FFMPEG`, the manifest unmarked with all 5 parts recorded. On that extract: `placements.ron` and a texture only the scene binds deleted, `guac-extract levels` read 2 parts and wrote 2 files, 641 kept, 3.52 s; the manifest set to layout 3 was rebuilt (`Rebuilt(Layout { found: 3 })`, the level read again in 11.18 s, still no ffmpeg); a run with ffmpeg back kept the level unread (5 parts, 0.17 s), transcoded 48 intros and marked it complete, and the next start found it `Current`. Each check broken in turn: pixel bit 6 or 9 dropped from a level's refusals, a level refusing the gloss map, a character taking it, a texture written whatever the store holds, the level stage moved after the intros or skipped without ffmpeg, a part done without its files or in the other format, a start current without its level, the layout back at 3: each failed at once. A level's vertex bits 6 and 9 dropped survived (the test asked only for vertex 11), so it asks for vertex 6 and 9 by name too and fails without them. Measured for decision 8's delay, off the dump: the archive read and parsed 2.5 s, the scene and props read 0.24 s, their RON 0.5 s; the rest of the stage's 10-11 s is AB's per-file `sync_all`, each file and each manifest save fsynced (the scene's and props' 696 texture files written alone: 7.57 s with it, 0.10 s without, at a load of 12); trading that durability for the first run's wait is AB's resume rule, so it's chotchki's call, not changed here. Fixed besides: `LevelStage::shared`'s doc said the characters', which on a resume counts the level's own textures too (289 of 289 on the 2-part resume), and `material::Track`'s doc said 4 tint tracks on the props where the disc has 3. No binary in the branch's diff (7 new files, all source), nothing tracked under an extract's extensions, the guard over the staged files, fmt, clippy -D warnings, test --workspace (1,316 passed, 0 failed, 112 ignored) and `openspec validate --all --strict` green; e2e: phone built with `BUILD_TOKEN=e2e-test`, `E2E_APP=guacamole` 57 passed, 0 failed, 310 s at a 1-minute load of 16.8-9.7)

Verify: the open-ground and wall cases also pass through the Avian side headless (AG.4's review, 2026-10-19, on what the seam leaves to this box: ag-motion section 1's rays are all `ray` (checkSupport's vertical check within 16 m, the ground finder from 0.1 m up doubling to 100 m then upward with its flip of a normal.y under −0.05, which a back-face hit's normal needs, the 0.375 m snap's height, the hug rays 2 m up and 4 m long), checkSupport's probe is a solve over the manifold, and each of the 10 casts is `contacts` at the start plus `cast`; Havok's proxy fills a cast collector beside the start points, so if AG.9's walls show more than the first hit mattering, the seam grows an all-hits cast); collision layers (the rays use layer 11, the proxy its own filter; the seam has exclude lists, enough while only the level and placements are solid); a ray starting inside a convex reports where it leaves (parry's non-solid ray), where Havok's convex rays probably report nothing (hypothesis): a hit whose normal points along the ray is the tell; a cast onto a rounded shape stops up to 1.6e-4 m inside it and a cast into what it starts in stops at 0, so the loop keeps Havok's keep distance rather than moving to the exact time of impact; Avian sees a moved kinematic body once the physics schedule has run (`FixedPostUpdate`), `ParryWorld` at once, so a body moved in `FixedUpdate` meets the hero a step later on the Avian side, which AG.18's two-sided replay shows unless moves land before the step; the open-ground and wall cases pass through Avian headless; each seam gap is handled in the controller or named below; one finding split to AG.48. **Built** ([`crates/guac-motion/src/`](../../../crates/guac-motion/src/lib.rs)), a module per family of mechanisms, each naming the hero-movement requirement its law rests on and labelling what the oracle didn't show: `hero`, a game frame in the original's order (Face and Move, the edge probe, Update Movers, the engine's move, the support check, the ground finder, the snap and the lean), the push and the facing on the step two frames before the frame's own, the slowing and gravity on the step one before, the move its own (AG.8, AG.38); `walk`, the stored walk in the script's ft/s with its vertical part (AG.38's switch at 21.5 + 1e-4 compared as the VM does, the push 160 x S along the stick tilted to the slope, the cut to the larger of 21.5 x S and the length it had, 80 ft/s² off the across part let go or over (50 in the air), the dead stop, the 0.4 ft band's 10 ft/s and cut, 136.21 ft/s² to 120 ft/s, the landing's slide; the facing at 1440°/s x S, frozen let go); `edge`, AG.36's probe (1.2105 m ahead over 2 ft/s, half the width and a foot along the stick under it, ground over the point or within 225 ft under it, refused: the across part zeroed before the move and the controller held off); `proxy`, the cast-and-slide (at most 10 casts, 5 mm keep, Havok's dedupe, the frictionless solve, no height from a wall, AG.9's wall law); `solve`; `ground` (the finder, support to 45°, the snap to 10 mm, the 120°/s lean); `solid` (shapes from a `CollisionInfo`, the 0.1 m shrink, 0.05 m convex radius, 0.001 m floor, the mesh's 0.05 m radius moved onto the hero's query shape); `stick`, `tuning`, `clock`; and, new here, `scenarios` (behind `conformance`): the open-ground and wall cases as a world of bodies and a check over any seam. **What the replays changed** (each found stepping the controller from a recording's own state, the record before as AG.38 did and free from a start): (a) an edge contact past the neighbour's face, which parry's internal-edge fix lets through (its cone test takes the nearest edge pseudo-normal, not the touched edge's), lifted him 3 mm at the start's 1.4° crease (triangles 2672 and 2428) in every walk off the spawn: `parry::planes_against` drops a mesh contact on an edge whose normal lies past the arc between the two faces' normals beyond the neighbour's, whose own face gives the plane (`seam::Mesh` keeps each triangle's neighbours, vertices matched by position); (b) a frame that fails the velocity model's leave test (0.01 m/s off the ground) loses support only when the walk also leaves faster than 0.01 of its own speed (the post-step's airborne test, as read): of AG.38's 632 such frames the two together call 608 as recorded, the leave test alone 97; (c) the support check reaches the read probe's travel, 1 m/s over 1/60 s: stepped a frame at a time from AG.38's 19,308 records, 8 mm past the skin calls 1,089 supported records unsupported, 1/60 m 165, 25 mm 143, the 93 without support called so at each; (d) the edge probe passes when a solid stands across the ground between his pivot and its point (AG.9's walls: the wall behind the start has nothing under or over the point behind it, and the original walks into it and slides); (e) the wall law runs while he's supported, laid along the ground or not (AG.9 at 20°: a walk leaving the 0.15° fall-away at 0.017 m/s slid at the law's 6.01 m/s), and the solve meets only planes he touches, the cast the rest, stopping him 5 mm off the hit plane by its own distance: he walks to a wall, then slides at the law's speed in the same frame, as AG.9's contact frames do (`ag9_wall_20` record 155: 0.0823 m and -0.0283 m from approach then slide against 0.0819 and -0.0276). the ground finder from inside a box over a floor finding the floor 2.1 m down, not the box's underside; the probe passing a wall at the level's edge and refusing the open side; the shapes as before (the push block, Norticus, Sheep_1 to 1e-5 m, Eruptor's 0.8644 m sphere 0.9144 m up, the gate door's 34 ft along x at heading 180 and along z without its own turn or with width and length swapped). 78 passed and the 10 `#[ignore]`d below run by hand (12:54, 1-minute load 3.21). **Against the recordings** ([`tests/replays.rs`](../../../crates/guac-motion/tests/replays.rs), 6 `#[ignore]`d, `GUAC_CAPTURE_DIR` and `GUAC_GIANTS_DUMP` from the env, else `.env.dev`; The four law replays (`recordings.rs`, now on the shared module) unchanged: the stored walk to 0.0316 ft/s on 19,288 records, the facing on level ground to 3.1e-5° on 3,667, AG.39's 244 free records to 1.8e-4 m/s, the cliff's 13 held faces held and 44 at 45.04° or more not, 26 landings called by the 30 ft/s rule and the band alike (the same run). **In `guacamole`** ([`crates/guacamole/src/hero.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/hero.rs)): `HeroPlugin` sets the fixed step to 60 Hz, adds `bevy_transform_interpolation`'s `TransformInterpolationPlugin` (the 0.6 avian3d already builds on, now a direct dependency, `std` only) and steps every `HeroBody` in `FixedUpdate` (`HeroSystems::Step`) over `AvianSeam` with `HeroInput` (the stick and the view) and `MovingSolids` (each moved body's velocity), writing his `Transform` (the pivot, his facing turned before his lean) with `TransformInterpolation`; not on the TV yet (AG.21's), so `expect(dead_code)` outside tests. Five tests, headless (`MinimalPlugins`, `SeamPlugin`, `HeroPlugin`, a 1/60 s fixed step): one step per 1/60 s display frame and the walk script's 120 steps on Avian equal to the controller stepped by hand on parry, 0 m apart at worst; at 30, 60 and 144 Hz display rates the same 120 steps leave him at (4.945127, 0.005, 4.945127), to the bit; at 144 Hz the drawn translation on the line from the step before's pivot to the step's at `overstep_fraction` within 1e-4 m on the 5 frames that ran a step, and off the pivot mid-step; a kinematic block moved after `HeroSystems::Step` pushing him at 1 m/s 5 mm off its face, 0 m from parry's; the 16 open-ground and wall scenarios through `AvianSeam`, all passing (12:54, 1-minute load 2.88 -> 2.75). **Hypotheses** (decision 4's list, each labelled in its module with the reading behind it): (1) no float creep past 21.5 + 1e-4: the free runs sit 0-1.1e-5 ft/s over at the gate's swing and all six turn free, where the original's three that sat 1.26e-4-1.49e-4 over steered into the door; (2) the push's tilt, the settled lean's slope or the ground finder's (AG.38's split to 0.05°); (3) the landing's slide, triggered from a last frame off the ground begun falling past 30 ft/s, not Falling and landing's last frame begun 0.4 ft up (the review's), at 1.2 x that fall x the slope/90° (AG.39's slides 1.08-4.75 m/s have no law); (4) the band on a walk rising past 20 ft/s and the 120 ft/s terminal (AG.47); (5) the probe's 1.2105 m, its slow point, its search the ground finder's, its 225 ft, nothing probed let go at 2 ft/s or under, the hold turning his facing; (6) the 10 casts and Havok's dedupe, the solve's planes within 0.1 m (Havok's keep-contact tolerance as a guess), a 1 m/s push off a plane he's inside, a moving surface carrying him along its normal at its speed and counted in the dedupe, its cast against its pose at the step's start; Found on the way: most of the level's layered materials set no values for their layers (no third layer sets a blend; of the 177 second layers, 174 set no blend and 148 no exponent), which AE's material refused, so they take the defaults the June state's draws read in every patched copy bar those that set their own (second layer over at exponent 1 and detail 0.25, third multiplied: `ag19/notes/layer_defaults.txt`, over ae-shader's savestate check), written into this change's character-shading delta as "Layers a level material leaves unset" with design decision 18's row. **The light**: `_worldLight1`-`3` off the lights part, each travelling the way its `tfbEulerTransform` faces (AF.9's convention, AE.13's reading of the same angles on the sprite rig), colours decoded as the remaster decodes them, each with its own ambient, no multiplier applied (1 on the disc); the point light `213_EvilRobot_Beam_light` (visible, at the origin, about 103 m from the start) with its linear and quadratic attenuation taken as the shader's two falloff terms and its constant dropped, a reading (ae-shader's open item), under 1 % of its colour at the start; no shadows (AG.23). The shader lights in view space, so the level keeps its lights in the level's frame and lights every material for its camera, again whenever the camera moves (the view's uniform rebuilt, each scroll kept). Fog: `LevelFog`, a resource every material's fog term reads, at the placeholder black from 10 km to 20 km, past camera 0's 243.84 m far clip, so it fogs nothing; on, since every level material keys fog. The sky's 16 segments reach 748.7 m out (x -748.7..584.9, y -320.1..325.6, z -632.6..527.8), past that far clip, and draw because Bevy's depth has no far plane and its culling keeps a segment the frustum touches; the original's far clip would cut them, so it draws its sky some other way (AG.13's capture can say). **The load** ([`level.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/level.rs)): the extraction tells the TV where chapter 1 is once a pick finds the extract current, or once the level stage has written every part (before ffmpeg is looked for, so a missing one doesn't keep it from the TV; on a first run that's after the transcode, behind which the first intro waits anyway). A task reads the scene and lights parts (checked to be this dump's and level's), builds the meshes, decodes the textures and keys the draws; then each frame hands the renderer at most 16 MiB of textures and meshes, then 64 materials; the warm compiles the level's keys meanwhile, each a static triangle on its hidden stage in the level's scene, no glow mask, queued behind what's asked; the level spawns hidden on its own render layer and is ready only once the render world has every texture, mesh (in the allocator's buffers) and material (its bind group) resident and the warm has every key compiled. `/api/_test/tv` gains `level` (state, counts, timings, why it failed). **The dev entry**: `GUAC_LEVEL_VIEW` in a `dev-tools` build shows the ready level over the TV through the remaster's camera into the TV's own target (the window or the shots' image), at AF's fresh-state camera: AG.7's first run's `default` viewport at F0 ((6.51073, 52.266262, -107.12627), tilt -23.359688, heading 14.99966) and AC.13's projection of the same pose (0.6 mm off: 53.96° top to bottom, near 0.1) with camera 0's 800 ft far clip; it replaces the TV rather than blending over it, since Bevy alpha-blends a second camera on a target and the level's opaque parts write their maps' alpha (the first shot showed the portal through them). **Unit tests in this box**, green: `level_material_keys_become_their_shader_defs` (the box's first: the level's kinds of masks, ignore 0x2FF, 0x3FF and 0x3BFF, in the level's scene, their defs, the gloss map's bit off on both sides and refused when kept, another effect drawing nothing), `the_warm_list_equals_the_levels_keys` (the box's second: the keys the level asks the warm for are each once exactly those its entities draw, material key on mesh shape, the grass's not among them), `the_level_is_ready_only_once_every_part_is_resident` (the box's third: waiting with all 11 parts pending, then one texture, then the warm alone, ready only once all are struck off and every key compiled, hidden until shown), `a_level_without_its_world_lights_fails_and_says_why` (and another dump's parts refused), `the_dev_view_shows_the_level_and_a_moved_camera_relights_it` (the camera's place, layer and replacing write, the lights relit for a turned camera, a scroll held at its start under reduced motion), `the_world_lights_travel_the_way_they_face_and_the_caustic_stays_out`, `a_camera_sees_the_lights_in_its_view_space_and_the_fog_from_the_level`, `a_layer_with_no_values_takes_the_defaults`, `a_scene_draws_its_tfbmulti2_segments_and_keys_them_once` (two scenes in one list), `the_fresh_state_camera_looks_where_the_capture_does` (forward and up to AC.13's to 1e-4, 53.96°), `a_static_segment_becomes_a_mesh_without_joints`, and the warm triangle's test over both skins; the guacamole lib 45 -> 52, the binary 244 -> 248. Each check this box adds bent in turn, 9 in all (the residency count, the warm's ask, the replacing write, the relight, the gloss bit, a layer's default, the light's turn into view, the point light's terms): each failed a test (the light's turn only the library's, since the dev view's test lights by the same function). **Measured** with a release build with test hooks (`cargo build --release -p guacamole --features test-hooks`: dev-tools on, so RON IR, the scene part 177 MB), desktop window on the built-in panel, `GUAC_MUTE=1`, `GUAC_LEVEL_VIEW=1`, chotchki's dump through `GUAC_DUMP_DIRS`, the extract this box's first, lost attempt made under `guacamole-research/extract/ag19-20261019T151409/` (current: IR 4, layout 4; removed after), driven by [`ag19/scripts/level.py`](/Users/chotchki/workspace/guacamole-research/ag19/scripts/level.py) (a stand-in phone, `inject_profile`, `POST /api/launch`, `/api/_test/tv` every 20 ms and the frame log), on a warm Metal shader cache for the level's keys (first compiled by this box's offscreen shot runs), runs in `ag19/runs/` (win-1, -2, -3, diag-1): pick to ready 1,867, 2,315, 2,313 and 1,860 ms (the parts read in 1.30-1.31 s, everything handed to the renderer by 1.46-1.55 s, the warm's 55 keys one a frame after that, so the 120 Hz runs finish sooner); the worst frame through the load 18.9, 25.2, 26.2 and 19.5 ms, 0 past 50 ms, the warm's 55 pipelines compiled in it; around the first show (0.5 s before to 2 s after) 53.0, 30.0, 29.7 and 20.9 ms, 1 frame past 50 ms, in win-1: the frame that first drew the level, which compiled one pipeline, the dev camera's blit to the window with a replacing write, new to this Mac's shader cache that run (diag-1's debug log: the blit alone compiled after the show; the level's keys all before it): AG.42. 1-minute load average 4.16-4.76 (3.25 for diag-1). AE.17's floor isn't measured yet (its box is open), so nothing sits beside it. **Beside the oracle** (`ag19/flip/`, outside the tree): `oracle-fresh-state.png` (AG.7's F0 shot, `captures/BLUS30968/ag7-level-1792410996/BLUS30968_screenshot_2026_10_19_04_56_46.png`, 1280x720) and `level-fresh-state.png` (`GUAC_SHOT_DIR` at 1280x720, shot2), `side-by-side.png`: by eye the layout agrees and nothing is mirrored, the bridge and its rails at the left, the cliff and the gateway beyond the flagstones where the screenshot's spiked gate stands (the gate's door is a prop, AG.25's, as are the boulder and gourds), the waterfall at the right in its frame, the rope fence and its posts across the front, the stump's hole where the boulder sits; ours reads darker and greyer than the oracle's warm frame (AC.13 found the runtime's rig replaced the disc's world lights at its pause: AG.41 checks it with the fog). LDR-FLIP at 67 ppd (the reference CLI, `flip-ref/cli-head`, bit-identical to `guac_oracle::frames::flip` per AF.10): mean 0.3763, weighted median 0.4671, quartiles 0.3111 and 0.7382, recorded, not gated (Eruptor, the HUD, the props, grass and the remaster's departure all in it). Gates: fmt, clippy --workspace --all-targets -D warnings, `cargo test --workspace` 1,427 passed + 121 ignored (the game-magic guard over the staged files among them; 1,416 before), `openspec validate --all --strict` 9 of 9; e2e: phone built with `BUILD_TOKEN=e2e-test`, `E2E_APP=guacamole` 60 of 60 ok, 360 s at a 1-minute load average of 3.5-12.2, the new `the_pick_loads_the_level_behind_the_portal` among them (the fixture's chapter 1 gains three world lights so it loads). CLAUDE.md's Dev mode gains `GUAC_LEVEL_VIEW` and `/api/_test/tv`'s `level`; the extract dir and every render outside the tree Review (2026-10-19, a second lane, M3 Max Mac): the load holds up and four defects are fixed. Off the frame as claimed: the scene and lights parts are read, the meshes built (`static_mesh`), the textures decoded (`remaster::texture_view`) and the keys made on an `AsyncComputeTaskPool` task, and the frame only adds what the task built, 16 MiB of textures and meshes or 64 materials at a time; the materials are AE's `CharacterMaterial::from_ir`, lit and loaded through the `remaster` module the turntable uses, and drawn through its camera; every new file is source, the game-magic guard green over the index. (1) The residency gate went unseen: the ready test struck off every part before any key compiled, so `wait` with its residency check deleted still passed (bent, green); the test now compiles every key first and waits on residency alone, the other order is `a_resident_level_waits_on_its_keys`, and the render world's strike-off is a function of its own under `the_render_world_strikes_off_only_what_is_resident`. (2) A second source while the first still waited kept the first's pending ids, whose handles had gone with it, so it could never be ready: a new source clears them (`a_new_source_waits_on_its_own_parts_alone`). (3) The fog wasn't a resource: a field of the loader, fixed at the placeholder, nothing to change it after the build; `LevelFog` is now the TV's resource, initialised to the placeholder, each material made with it, a change relighting them all (`a_changed_fog_reaches_every_level_material`), so AG.41 sets one value (savestate-oracle's section 4 already has the June state's tutorial fog, (115, 83, 49) from 30.48 to 335.28 m on 2,397 copies, a check for AG.13's reading). One reading stated as more: the layer defaults' note (`ag19/notes/layer_defaults.txt`) tallies the June state's patched copies per variant without joining them to materials, so "bar those that set their own" is inferred, not shown: the third layer's multiply is on every copy, the second layer's over on most (0x1 3,890 of 5,166 copies, 0x41B 234 of 245), the rest unattributed. (4) The dev entry's first frame was lit for the identity: the relight ran in `Update` straight after the camera spawned, before its transform propagated, so the frame that first drew the level lit every material with the world's light directions as the view's and the next frame relit all 471 again; it now runs in `PostUpdate` after propagation and before the assets' events, from the fresh state's view as Bevy propagates it, and the show frame rewrites no material (the dev view's test checks both; it failed on the branch's code). Each check bent in turn failed its test: the residency gate, the keys gate, the stale ids, the strike's sense, the fog's change and its value, the relight back in the chain, a wrong lit-for. Measured again with the fixes (release with test hooks, `GUAC_LEVEL_VIEW`, AG.19's driver over a fresh extract under `guacamole-research/extract/`, removed after; `ag19/review/runs/`): pick to ready 2,313, 2,349 and 2,282 ms; the worst frame through the load 28.5, 24.6 and 24.8 ms and around the first show 28.5, 25.6 and 27.9 ms, none past 50, the display at 60 Hz, 1-minute load average 7.8-10.6; the slow frame just after the show 26.9, 25.6 and 25.3 ms where win-2 and win-3 had 30.0 and 29.7, so the double relight cost a few ms at most and AG.42's 53.0 ms stays the blit's. The extract run, a first run: the level reached the TV 103.6 s after the pick (after the transcode, as above) and was ready 3.7 s later; that run's worst frames, 196-225 ms, came in the turntables' warm under the extraction (1-minute load 15.4), AE.25's. Left for AG.21, not fixed: the materials light in view space, so a camera that moves rewrites every level material each frame (471 materials prepared again a frame); AG.21's following camera wants the lights in a per-view binding, and its walk measurement will show the cost if not. Gates: fmt, clippy --workspace --all-targets -D warnings, `cargo test --workspace` 1,443 passed + 122 ignored (the binary's 249 -> 253), `openspec validate --all --strict` 9 of 9; e2e: phone built with `BUILD_TOKEN=e2e-test`, `E2E_APP=guacamole` 60 of 60 in 5 min 26 s at a 1-minute load average of 4.3-10.0, `the_pick_loads_the_level_behind_the_portal` among them)

Then `guac-motion`'s `Stick::push` in the hero's step: pad-input's per-axis dead zone, rescale and cap, the direction against `FollowCamera`'s view (AG.17's). **The corner**: read as sent, the gate not squared, labelled a hypothesis in `pad.rs` until AG.5's gate (AG.49): pad-input's Between the axes is a round gate's rim read as is, a round rim's diagonal (0.7071 an axis) walks at 0.8619 of top speed, and Full on both axes holds on a pad that reads full on both. **The seat** (`Seat`, pure): the first pad connected walks (`LevelPads` keeps the connection order); another's stick and faces move no one; the walking pad's faces by position into the hero's `Input::buttons` (bottom, right, left, top); Start on any pad pauses under the one-button overlay, and the bottom button or Start on any pad resumes; the walking pad lost, or none at the level's start, waits under the controller message until any button on any pad (Home too, if it arrives) takes the hero over; Select and Home do nothing; a press the layer took (the resume, the take-over) stays off the hero until let go. **The clock** ([`pause.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/pause.rs)): the frame the level comes to wait runs the fixed steps already due on the pad as the frame before read it, then `Time<Virtual>` stops (no fixed step, the drawn transform and the scrolls held) until it carries on, the resume's own frame stepping nothing. **The overlays**: TV UI at z 900 (an intro's is 1,000) drawn with the TV's `Pen`: "Paused" over "Press [glyph] to play on", the glyph the bottom button as printed on the pad that paused; or "Connect a controller" over "then press any button on it". Headless: `a_press_under_an_intro_skips_it_and_moves_no_one` (over the level a South skips the intro, the hero reads nothing in its frames and the South stays off him until pressed again); `a_pad_walks_him_and_start_or_losing_it_stops_everything` (the router and the hero on Avian: 40 steps forward along the view within 1e-2°; Start stopping every step and the drawn transform for 60 frames while the stick moves; the bottom button carrying on one step later at the speed he had, to 1e-3 m/s; the pad switched off stopping him, another's stick taking nothing over in 30 frames and its East taking him over along 105° within 1°; the level hidden letting the clock go); `posted_pads_drive_the_level_as_a_hand_would` (one act a body, seven bad bodies a 400; a posted pad's raw stick; it walking once the level shows; Start's overlay as drawn, "Paused", "Press", "to play on" and `position:south`; the controller message on its disconnection; another pad's East taking over). 16 new; guacamole's binary 283 to 299 passed. **Bent**, each alone over these tests (`ag20/mut/run.py` in the session's scratchpad): 21 bends, each failing at least one: the raw settings off, or set after Bevy's event system; the hero reading the first pad rather than the walking one (survived until the take-over test read B's stick with A back ahead of it); the pausing frame reading nothing; any button resuming; a stick taking over; nothing swallowed; a lost pad walking on; only the walking pad's Start pausing; Select pausing; the clock not stopping; the level over the intro; the Nintendo letters swapped; an unknown pad given Xbox letters; the overlay's prompt from the walking pad; the faces out of order; the hero keeping a stale stick under an intro; and in `guac-motion`'s stick the dead zone at a fifth, no rescale past it, a radial dead zone and no cap. Who walks is the library's [`walkers.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/walkers.rs): Eruptor's load file on `Tuning::ERUPTOR` and his `CollisionInfo` (a 4 x 6 x 3 ft sphere) at 150 %, AG.8's live reads, now `guac_motion::tuning`'s; any other figure, a character placed before him included, keeps the portal view and its card (Open question 1's (a) until AG.27), and Eruptor placed after it still walks in. The TV enters once no intro plays or waits, the screen is the portal view, the start's pass is done, the level is ready and his card's model (the turntable's own read, off the shelf on a task) has every key it draws compiled, the warm asked for any that aren't; until then the portal view holds. The level now comes with its collision ([`level.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/level.rs)): the load's task also reads the collision and placements parts (checked to be this dump's and level's) and builds each world's mesh into an Avian trimesh with its internal edges fixed there (`PreparedBody`, so the frame only spawns it, 3,080 triangles on chapter 1), and Avian puts a collider in its trees, its bounds computed, as it's inserted, so he settles on it the frame it spawns (a guard waiting a physics step for it changed nothing a test could see, and went). He starts at `213_Init_Location`'s first waypoint (`level_scene::start_point`) facing 0°, settled by AG.16's controller on Avian's side ([`hero.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/hero.rs)'s `HeroPlugin`, now on the TV), his card's model hanging on him at 150 % on the level's layer, idling on AE's evaluator on the TV's virtual clock (held under reduced motion and through a pause). He's drawn in the level's zone and fog without its point light (`level_scene::hero_scene`): his masks are then the ones his card draws (fog on, nothing else), so his keys are the census's, and `giants_census` now holds each walker's keys in that scene inside the card tables on the dump (Eruptor's 1 key, 0 outside; 42.6 s, debug, 1-minute load 2.98 -> 2.24). The camera is camera 0 at rest on his feet, AG.17's follow, no planes, spawned through `level::view_camera` on the TV's target at the level's order with its replacing write (AG.44) and following him eased (`hero::following`, its projection camera 0's 53.96°, 0.1 m and far clip); the level's layer takes the pads (`LevelPads::show`). The TV leaves the level when its screen leaves the portal view or the level gives way to another load: hero and camera gone, the pads the screen's, the level hidden and ready again. A removal, a second character, an item or an intro once the level shows are AG.29's: today the hero walks on, the cards stay under the level and an intro plays under the level's camera. `/api/_test/tv` gains `mode` (`portal`, `level`, `paused`) and `hero` (slot, figure, load file, his model's way to the level, and in the level his `position` and `heading`), and the level report `triangles`, `start` and `relit`; `GUAC_SHOT_WALK=<ms>` shoots the walk as a sequence (off by default: a shot's PNG is written on the frame's thread). **Found, and fixed here**: the level lit in view space (AG.19's review left it to this box) costs the walk 60 frames a second. Measured first on that build (`cold-1`, `warm-1`, below) the level ran at a 19.0 ms median frame standing as well as walking, against 16.7 ms shown and waiting: every frame the camera moved `light_level` rewrote all 471 of chapter 1's materials and the hero's, and at rest it still did, the eased camera's rotation jittering by float noise (131 of 144 frames at 144 Hz, headless, with the eye and his pivot unmoved). The character uniform's `misc.w` now says which frame its lights are in ([`material.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/character/material.rs) `LIGHTS_IN_WORLD`, [`shading.wesl`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/character/shading.wesl) `light_travel` and `point_position`): 1 hands the shader the directional lights' travels and the point lights' positions in the world's frame and it turns them by `view.view_from_world`, 0 keeps a turntable's rig in view space, as before. The level's and the hero's materials are made lit in the world's frame (`level_scene::world_scene`) and relit only when the fog changes; on that build the walk ran at 16.6-16.7 ms median frames, and the start's frame (`shots/cold-2/04-Portal-1cards-Ready.png` against `shots/cold-1/56-Portal-1cards-Ready.png`, the same pose) differs in 14,353 of its 6,220,800 channels, by 1 but for 60, which are 21 pixels on edges (32 at most), so the GPU turns the world's lights as the CPU did. **Unit tests in this box** (`cargo test -p guacamole`, headless, made-up data): the box's three, `a_character_placed_enters` (Eruptor placed with the level loaded: the level shows through the following camera, order 1 with the replacing write on the level's layer, the pads the level's; the first pad's stick walks him along the camera's 15° within 0.05° and the made-up wall 6 m ahead holds him 5 mm off it, his 0.8644 m sphere on the triangles' 0.05 m, while he slides on along it past 3 m; its FINDINGS section 4 names only over and multiply), the three modes put through k, the rim strength the gloss leaves; Full gloss's WHEN gains "at strength 1", since a gloss amount is the red times the map's strength. **Model** ([`guac_ir::shading`](../../../crates/guac-ir/src/shading.rs)): `Rim::gloss`, the gloss map's strength (the material's fixed alpha for the third layer); the gloss is the third layer's texture's red as sampled, at the set a third layer would take (`uv_sets`); a material with a gloss map and a third layer refused (`Error::GlossAndThirdLayer`); `Switches::gloss`. **Shader and material**: [`key.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/character/key.rs) takes bit 7 (needs the rim, `Requires { bit: 7, needs: 2 }`; never with bit 1, `Excludes`), its def `CHAR_GLOSS_MAP` and the third set (`CHAR_UV1`, `CHAR_UV2` beside a second layer); `reference_material` sets the rim's gloss from the fixed alpha's z, which the uniform carries in a third layer's place; On the Mac: the gate's door, the gourds and the boulder where the oracle's screenshot has them, and the door stopping Eruptor at about AG.7's distance (AG.31 compares it) (2026-10-21, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3, the screen locked: chapter 1's props stand by AG.7's rule, drawn from their models in the level's look and solid on both sides of the seam; on the TV the gate's door, the gourds and the boulder stand where the oracle's fresh-state screenshot has them, and the door stops Eruptor with his pivot 1.47809 m from its middle, the oracle's 1.4781, at z −83.70229, the oracle's to the digit. What the start's scripts do to the props (the placements they wake, the solids and scales they rewrite) is split to AG.60; a crease on the clearing that pins the controller, props or not, found on the way, to AG.61. **Built** ([`guacamole::props`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/props.rs), the library's): each placement through AG.7's rule as guac-formats holds it (`at_start_of`, the rule on what the extract keeps): a template never stands; a creature (a skinned model with a script: enemies, NPCs, critters, the chests) is left out with its solid; one without a range waits on its script, which nothing runs yet; the rest stand while the hero is within their range, 3D from the placement's position, the boundary in. A standing prop draws its model's tfbmulti2 segments on their materials as the level's draw (`level_scene::drawn`, the warp's left out, AG.24's), placed by its position, Euler angles (AF.9's convention) and scale; a skinned model with no script, or one the stage refused, draws nothing (none on chapter 1). Its solid is guac-motion's `HavokSolid` off its `CollisionInfo` at its scale (AG.7's table), posed by its own angles then the placement's and moved 0.05 m onto the hero's query shape (`in_world`), a solid one a kinematic seam body, a non-solid one a sensor, a shapeless or mesh-typed one nothing; ids `PROP_BODIES` (2²⁰) plus the object index. The TV's loader ([`level.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/level.rs)) reads the props part beside the walk's on its task, builds each solid into an Avian collider there, puts the props' materials after the level's (so the fog's relight and the scrolls take them) and their meshes after its segments; `StandingProps` spawns each prop's draw hidden under the level's root and stands the props by the hero's pivot in `FixedUpdate` before his step (by the start until he's in): a draw shown or hidden, a solid spawned into Avian's world or despawned from it. `/api/_test/tv`'s level carries `props` (what can stand, what stands now and where from, each by name, what's left out by why). guac-motion's `ParryWorld` takes the same bodies (`LevelProps::bodies`). **IR v6**: a level's placement keeps its `_activationRange` (`activation_range`, feet), which a v5 placements file can't say, so every extract redoes itself once (`LAYOUT_VERSION` 4 kept); the stage writes it, its test reads the builder's 160 ft back. **Chapter 1** (`giants_props`, on the dump through the extractor's readers in memory, release, 2.5 s, 1-minute load 4.1): 313 of 997 placements can stand, on 46 models; left out 413 templates, 110 creatures, 98 by script, 63 with nothing to stand or draw; at AF's fresh state (AF.9's idle pivot) 37 stand, 33 drawn, 26 solid and 3 sensing. Every reader it named reads what the VM now runs, so each would be a second watch on a number the VM computes. The camera definitions, volumes, planes and the camera system's defaults are `Level_Definition_213.ai`'s and `global.bld`'s, which the extract already carries whole (AH.22's `script` and `scripts` parts) and AG.28 runs; the planes' and volumes' boxes are their placements' `CollisionInfo`, which AG.15's placements carry. Each character's tuning links and locomotion calls are his archive's setup scripts over `Init_Setup.ai`'s boot tuning and `global.bld`'s `Minion_Tuning.ai` (both on the VM already, [`scripts.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/scripts.rs)), which AG.27 runs once the extract carries his archive's entry: AH.22's `FIGHTERS`, Eruptor alone, widened, parsed by AH.13's op-graph readers, so no reader of AG's own. In AG.34's walk-W-06 the ring's record at flip 1,729 (read 0, game frame 7,690, 191 ms after the record before it, the game frames one apart throughout) has no O4 count (`PAD_STATE 0 1729` answered `no_read`) and flip 1,730 has no record; the runner asks O4 only about flips the ring holds, so whether O4 kept that read under 1,730 or not at all isn't known. `RingCheck` counted it (`reads_mismatch` 1, `holds` false): 1 read of AG.34's 18,000 watched, none of AG.35's 36,600. A record's guest memory (pose, stick floats, `_curFrame`) is read at that read whatever its flip says; what a split key moves is O4's readback joined to the record (`pad` none there) and any script step mapped to a game frame through a record's flip. Options: take the counter once at the top of `pad_get_data` and hand it to both (O5c, a new build); or put O4's flip in O5's header beside its own. Either way, the runner asks O4 about every flip in the ring's span, so a read O4 kept under the next flip, or never kept, shows as which. Its loopback case: a flip ticking between the two loads. Closes before AG.8 takes a frame's input from O4's readback or from a script's flips rather than from the record's own stick floats. On the three planes (2 each): walking down 1.32-2.54 m/s, across 0.01-0.52, up 1.21-2.73, each uphill slide leaving the ground for a frame (whose 10 ft/s turns the upward vertical part down), 0.81-1.05 m in 0.23-0.27 s; the vertical part is the slope's (6.39 ft/s, 21.5 × sin 17.3°, walking down t2979) or a crest's. A macOS-only pad source in `guacamole` (the objc2 family's `objc2-game-controller` 0.3.2 with `objc2-foundation` 0.3.2 and `block2` 0.6, on the objc2 0.6 the tree already locks) feeding Bevy's own `RawGamepadEvent` stream as bevy_gilrs and AG.20's `/api/_test/tv/pad` do, so `pad.rs`, the router, the pause and the hero read pads unchanged; bevy_gilrs off on macOS (one source per pad), kept for Windows. Buttons by position per product category: the Switch Pro as AG.5 measured it (bottom Apple's B, right A, left Y, top X), any other category Apple's default (A at the bottom, B right, X left, Y top), unmeasured and labelled so in the code; L and R, ZL and ZR, plus (Start), minus (Select), Home (Mode), the stick clicks and the D-pad by role. Sticks as Apple delivers them, nothing of ours before guac-motion's `Stick::push`. Each pad's input-state queue drained in order every frame on the main thread (a `NonSend` system ahead of Bevy's input systems), so a press and its release inside one frame still reach the router as a press; a pad on an OS without the queue read once a frame, with a warning. The prompt resolver on macOS takes each position's letter from the element Apple names there (its SF Symbol, else its name), so the Switch-mode pad's prompt is B; shapes or a mix give the position glyph. Each frame: `GCController.controllers()` in the framework's order, a pad gone sending its disconnection (its entity kept, as Bevy keeps a gamepad's), a new one opened (vendorName as the pad's name, no ids; a controller without an extended profile left alone and asked once), its live input's queue set to 128 readings (`QUEUE_DEPTH`, about two seconds of a Bluetooth pad's reports) with a no-op input-state handler and drained of anything stale (a queue's worth at most), Home's system gesture asked off (`preferredSystemGestureState` disabled on its profile element; Apple documents it as a preference the system needn't respect, and at AG.51 macOS opened its Games app on Home anyway, the backlog's Home menu line), then every reading queued since the last frame diffed in order into button and axis events (a drain capped at 256 a frame against a framework that never runs dry). Element lookups by Apple's input names (`GCInputButtonA` and the rest, the keys AG.5's probe saw); where the OS has no `input` (before macOS 14) or the live input lacks the four faces by those names, the profile is read once a frame and a warning says a press shorter than a frame can be missed. `shouldMonitorBackgroundEvents` on, so pads read whether or not guacamole's window is in front, as gilrs reads them on Windows. **Layouts** (`LAYOUTS`): `Switch Pro Controller` measured (AG.5: bottom Apple's B, right A, left Y, top X); any other category `APPLE_DEFAULT` (A, B, X, Y from the bottom round), `measured: None` and said so in the code and the connection's log line. buttons 1.0 or 0.0 from `isPressed`, sticks as Apple's floats, x right and y up, nothing of ours in between. **The resolver** ([`pad.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/pad.rs)): the backend puts `FacesPrinted` (the letter at bottom, right, left and top, from each element's SF Symbol, `b.circle`, else its name, `B Button`; none for a shape or a name without a letter) on the pad's entity; `labels` takes Xbox's or Nintendo's when all four letters are exactly one of them, else the position, and the id table only for a pad without `FacesPrinted`; `GcPads` holds a `Box<dyn Controllers>` and `apple.rs` holds `Retained` framework objects, neither `Send`, so it can only be a `NonSend` resource; nothing Objective-C leaves `apple.rs` (it hands out strings, letters and `PadState`s) and the input-state handler captures nothing. **Retain and release**: every object a `Retained`, the handler block copied by its setter, a closed controller's objects dropped with it. **A pad coming and going mid-frame**: the framework delivers its connections on the main queue, which runs between frames, so the list a frame reads holds through the system; a new pad's first reading is its profile's, then its queue; what a pad sent in the frame it disconnects is dropped, as Bevy drops a gone pad's events that frame anyway (bevy_gilrs's too), now said in the module docs. **gilrs**: `cargo tree -p guacamole -e normal -i gilrs` lists it for `aarch64-apple-darwin` as for Windows and Linux (Bevy's `ui` collection builds bevy_gilrs in), `objc2-game-controller` only for macOS; The test is there for the refusal (`failed`, "the scene part is another dump's or level's"), not the frame between, so the fix keeps the refusal's check and stops asserting a frame the task can skip; `a_new_source_waits_on_its_own_parts_alone` (:2568) and the walk's level-swap test (:2894) assert the same frame over a whole level's read, a longer task, and get the same look. Unit tests in this box: none new; the changed tests are the check. ## Workflow follow-up

- Archive only after AA and AE have archived: AG's figure-cards and figure-turntable deltas MODIFY requirements that live in their unarchived deltas, and its character-shading delta adds to AE's unarchived capability, so `openspec archive` would refuse AG first. A MODIFIED block replaces the whole requirement at archive, so before AG archives each one is rebuilt from AA's and AE's archived text with AG's scoping applied, and diffed against what AG carries, so no later AA or AE edit (AE.13's, say) is silently reverted (AG.0 records the diff).
- AE.13 (the turntables on the cards) and AG.29 (the level taking the TV from the cards) change the same screen; whichever lands second keeps the other's tests passing.
